Complete reference: 170 terms and shapes · All vocabularies and reading guide · Generated labels, definitions and scope notes (Turtle): media-encoding-doc.ttl

Specification family

Hexplain Register — Media Encoding

The vocabulary module publishing Hexplain Register — Media Encoding terms, its imports and associated validation declarations.

Unofficial working specification. The namespace and version metadata below identify the vocabulary; documentation does not certify an implementation.

Complete term and shape reference

170 named resources are documented below. Definitions and usage notes come from the canonical RDF; range and validation facts are rendered from its axioms and shapes. RDFS/OWL inference, SHACL validation, and editorial usage guidance are shown separately. Blank-node property shapes and logical alternatives are expanded under their owning named shapes.

Constraint language follows W3C SHACL; publication and persistent term identifiers follow the W3C vocabulary publication guidance. Concept definitions and scope notes use SKOS documentation properties.

lib.contract.trailingmenc:Zstdmenc:Zstd.contract

Shared usage scopes

Shared scope 1
Read by processors and conformance tools to bind and check the hexplain:codecParameter values of an encoding step that names the concept. It is not a controlled value: profiles name the concept, never its contract.
Ontology

<https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding> — Hexplain Register — Media Encoding

IRI: https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding

Label
Hexplain Register — Media Encoding
Definition
The vocabulary module publishing Hexplain Register — Media Encoding terms, its imports and associated validation declarations.
Usage scope
Load to obtain the concepts of this register. A profile binds its schemes to the properties they supply with hexplain:usesRegister; the aspect the values serve does not import it. Codec/algorithm concepts require any applicable variant, framing and parameter information from the profile.
Declared RDF type
owl:Ontology
Domain / range
Not applicable to this ontology. Domain and range describe predicates; the types, relationships and constraints below describe this resource.

menc:BitReverse — Bit reversal

IRI: https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding#BitReverse

Label
Bit reversal
Definition
A reversible transform that reverses the order of the bits within every byte, converting between least- and most-significant-bit-first fill orders.
Declared RDF type
skos:Concept
Domain / range
Not applicable to this concept. Domain and range describe predicates; the types, relationships and constraints below describe this resource.
Concept scheme
menc:TransformScheme
Defined by
<https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding>
Shape usage / validation context
No direct named family-shape reference found. This is not a claim that a profile cannot add constraints or reference this term indirectly.
Cardinality
No universal cardinality declared for this term.
Additional source notes
Reverses the order of the bits within every byte (TIFF FillOrder 2 to FillOrder 1). Its own inverse.
Canonical source
media-encoding.ttl

Concept

menc:BitRound — Bit round

IRI: https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding#BitRound

Label
Bit round
Definition
A lossy floating-point filter that discards the low-order mantissa bits beyond a kept precision when encoding; decoding is the identity.
Declared RDF type
skos:Concept
Domain / range
Not applicable to this concept. Domain and range describe predicates; the types, relationships and constraints below describe this resource.
Concept scheme
menc:TransformScheme
Defined by
<https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding>
Shape usage / validation context
No direct named family-shape reference found. This is not a claim that a profile cannot add constraints or reference this term indirectly.
Cardinality
No universal cardinality declared for this term.
Additional source notes
numcodecs' BitRound filter, which rounds float mantissas to keepbits bits when encoding; decoding is the identity.
Canonical source
media-encoding.ttl
Declared axioms and values
  • rdf:type: skos:Concept
  • skos:inScheme: menc:TransformScheme
  • skos:prefLabel: "Bit round"@en
  • skos:topConceptOf: menc:TransformScheme
  • hexplain:codecContract: menc:BitRound.contract
  • hexplain:status: hexplain:statusValid

menc:BloscLZ — BloscLZ

IRI: https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding#BloscLZ

Label
BloscLZ
Definition
The LZ77-style block compressor built into the Blosc framework, with 13-bit match distances and escaped longer ones.
Declared RDF type
skos:Concept
Domain / range
Not applicable to this concept. Domain and range describe predicates; the types, relationships and constraints below describe this resource.
Concept scheme
menc:CompressionScheme
Defined by
<https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding>
Shape usage / validation context
No direct named family-shape reference found. This is not a claim that a profile cannot add constraints or reference this term indirectly.
Cardinality
No universal cardinality declared for this term.
Additional source notes
c-blosc's BloscLZ compressor (c-blosc 1.21.6 blosclz_decompress): literal runs of 1-32 bytes and matches at a 13-bit distance or, escaped, beyond 8191. Decodes at most decodedSize bytes; as in C, a match that is the stream's last instruction is not copied.
Canonical source
media-encoding.ttl
Declared axioms and values
  • rdf:type: skos:Concept
  • skos:inScheme: menc:CompressionScheme
  • skos:prefLabel: "BloscLZ"@en
  • skos:topConceptOf: menc:CompressionScheme
  • hexplain:codecContract: menc:BloscLZ.contract
  • hexplain:status: hexplain:statusValid

menc:CCITTGroup3 — CCITT Group 3

IRI: https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding#CCITTGroup3

Label
CCITT Group 3
Definition
ITU-T T.4 (Group 3) facsimile run-length coding of bilevel rows, one- or two-dimensional, each row introduced by an EOL code.
Declared RDF type
skos:Concept
Domain / range
Not applicable to this concept. Domain and range describe predicates; the types, relationships and constraints below describe this resource.
Concept scheme
menc:CompressionScheme
Defined by
<https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding>
Shape usage / validation context
No direct named family-shape reference found. This is not a claim that a profile cannot add constraints or reference this term indirectly.
Cardinality
No universal cardinality declared for this term.
Additional source notes
CCITT T.4 (Group 3) facsimile coding, one- or two-dimensional as bit 0 of options (the T4Options tag) says, each row after an EOL code unless noEol, decoded with libtiff's recovery rules; the reference line carries across strips in the session.

menc:CCITTModifiedHuffman — CCITT Modified Huffman

IRI: https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding#CCITTModifiedHuffman

Label
CCITT Modified Huffman
Definition
One-dimensional ITU-T T.4 modified Huffman run-length coding of bilevel rows without EOL codes, each row starting on a byte or word boundary.
Declared RDF type
skos:Concept
Domain / range
Not applicable to this concept. Domain and range describe predicates; the types, relationships and constraints below describe this resource.
Concept scheme
menc:CompressionScheme
Defined by
<https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding>
Shape usage / validation context
No direct named family-shape reference found. This is not a claim that a profile cannot add constraints or reference this term indirectly.
Cardinality
No universal cardinality declared for this term.
Additional source notes
One-dimensional CCITT T.4 run-length coding of bilevel rows without EOL codes, each row starting on a byte (rowAlignment 8, TIFF compression 2) or 16-bit word (rowAlignment 16, TIFF compression 32771) boundary, decoded with libtiff's recovery rules. Output: rows of ceil(columns / 8) bytes, most significant bit first, 1 for black.
Canonical source
media-encoding.ttl
Declared axioms and values
  • rdf:type: skos:Concept
  • skos:inScheme: menc:CompressionScheme
  • skos:prefLabel: "CCITT Modified Huffman"@en
  • skos:topConceptOf: menc:CompressionScheme
  • hexplain:codecContract: menc:CCITTModifiedHuffman.contract
  • hexplain:status: hexplain:statusValid
Named individual

menc:Delta — Delta / predictor

IRI: https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding#Delta

Label
Delta / predictor
Definition
A predictive transform that represents values by differences from preceding or predicted values; the variant and arithmetic are profile parameters.
Declared RDF type
skos:Concept
Domain / range
Not applicable to this concept. Domain and range describe predicates; the types, relationships and constraints below describe this resource.
Concept scheme
menc:CompressionScheme
Defined by
<https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding>
Shape usage / validation context
No direct named family-shape reference found. This is not a claim that a profile cannot add constraints or reference this term indirectly.
Cardinality
No universal cardinality declared for this term.
Notes / examples
"Stores each value as its difference from the previous one. TIFF calls this a horizontal predictor and numbers its variants; the variant travels as a parameter. Its parameters are named by the Processing Model (Codecs, Delta parameters): elementSize (required: 1, 2, 4 or 8), littleEndian (0 or 1), samplesPerPixel (default 1), rowBytes (0 or a multiple of elementSize x samplesPerPixel) and predictor (2, horizontal, by default; 1, none)."@en
Canonical source
media-encoding.ttl
Declared axioms and values
  • rdf:type: skos:Concept
  • skos:inScheme: menc:CompressionScheme
  • skos:note: "Stores each value as its difference from the previous one. TIFF calls this a horizontal predictor and numbers its variants; the variant travels as a parameter. Its parameters are named by the Processing Model (Codecs, Delta parameters): elementSize (required: 1, 2, 4 or 8), littleEndian (0 or 1), samplesPerPixel (default 1), rowBytes (0 or a multiple of elementSize x samplesPerPixel) and predictor (2, horizontal, by default; 1, none)."@en
  • skos:prefLabel: "Delta / predictor"@en
  • skos:topConceptOf: menc:CompressionScheme
  • hexplain:codecContract: menc:Delta.contract
  • hexplain:status: hexplain:statusValid

menc:FixedScaleOffset — Fixed scale and offset

IRI: https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding#FixedScaleOffset

Label
Fixed scale and offset
Definition
A sample transform that restores floating-point values from samples stored as a narrower integer type by dividing by a fixed scale and adding an offset.
Declared RDF type
skos:Concept
Domain / range
Not applicable to this concept. Domain and range describe predicates; the types, relationships and constraints below describe this resource.
Concept scheme
menc:TransformScheme
Defined by
<https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding>
Shape usage / validation context
No direct named family-shape reference found. This is not a claim that a profile cannot add constraints or reference this term indirectly.
Cardinality
No universal cardinality declared for this term.
Additional source notes
numcodecs' FixedScaleOffset filter decoded as GDAL does: astype values widened to dtype, then value / scale + offset in double precision (rounded to binary32 for <f4).

menc:FloatingPointPredictor — Floating-point predictor

IRI: https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding#FloatingPointPredictor

Label
Floating-point predictor
Definition
A reversible transform that regroups the bytes of floating-point samples by significance and differences them, undone before the samples are reassembled.
Declared RDF type
skos:Concept
Domain / range
Not applicable to this concept. Domain and range describe predicates; the types, relationships and constraints below describe this resource.
Concept scheme
menc:TransformScheme
Defined by
<https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding>
Shape usage / validation context
No direct named family-shape reference found. This is not a claim that a profile cannot add constraints or reference this term indirectly.
Cardinality
No universal cardinality declared for this term.
Additional source notes
Adobe TIFF Technical Note 3's floating-point predictor: each row's sample bytes regrouped by significance (every sample's most significant byte first) and differenced byte by byte, each byte predicted from the byte samplesPerPixel positions earlier. Decoding sums the differences and reassembles the samples in the byte order littleEndian names.
Canonical source
media-encoding.ttl
Declared axioms and values
  • rdf:type: skos:Concept
  • skos:inScheme: menc:TransformScheme
  • skos:prefLabel: "Floating-point predictor"@en
  • skos:topConceptOf: menc:TransformScheme
  • hexplain:codecContract: menc:FloatingPointPredictor.contract
  • hexplain:status: hexplain:statusValid

menc:JPEG — JPEG

IRI: https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding#JPEG

Label
JPEG
Definition
ITU-T T.81 (JPEG) DCT-based lossy image coding, in its baseline, extended and progressive Huffman-coded forms.
Declared RDF type
skos:Concept
Domain / range
Not applicable to this concept. Domain and range describe predicates; the types, relationships and constraints below describe this resource.
Concept scheme
menc:CodecScheme
Defined by
<https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding>
Shape usage / validation context
No direct named family-shape reference found. This is not a claim that a profile cannot add constraints or reference this term indirectly.
Cardinality
No universal cardinality declared for this term.
Additional source notes
ITU-T T.81 baseline, extended (12-bit) and progressive Huffman-coded JPEG, decoded with libjpeg's integer IDCT and upsampling. tables supplies an abbreviated table-specification stream (TIFF JPEGTables) kept in the session for every later image; colorTransform ycbcr-to-rgb converts as libjpeg does; rows stops after that many rows; output raw-planes returns the component planes before upsampling and conversion.
Canonical source
media-encoding.ttl

menc:LERC — LERC

IRI: https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding#LERC

Label
LERC
Definition
Esri Limited Error Raster Compression, a bounded-error coding of integer and floating-point raster samples with a validity mask per band.
Declared RDF type
skos:Concept
Domain / range
Not applicable to this concept. Domain and range describe predicates; the types, relationships and constraints below describe this resource.
Concept scheme
menc:CodecScheme
Defined by
<https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding>
Shape usage / validation context
No direct named family-shape reference found. This is not a claim that a profile cannot add constraints or reference this term indirectly.
Cardinality
No universal cardinality declared for this term.
Additional source notes
Esri's Limited Error Raster Compression version 2 (blob versions 1-6), decoded bit for bit as Esri LERC 4.0 decodes it. layout blob returns every band's samples band after band with a validity mask per band; layout tiff assembles one TIFF strip or tile of width x height x samplesPerPixel samples as libtiff's tif_lerc.c does (masks become NaN for floats or alpha when alphaFromMask), first unwrapping the Deflate or Zstandard layer lercParameters names.

PackBits

Additional source notes
Byte-oriented run-length coding (TIFF 6.0 section 9): a signed control byte n copies n+1 literal bytes (0 <= n <= 127), repeats the next byte 1-n times (-127 <= n <= -1), or does nothing (-128).

menc:TypedDelta — Typed delta

IRI: https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding#TypedDelta

Label
Typed delta
Definition
A reversible transform storing each element of a typed numeric array as its difference from the previous one, undone by a running sum in that type.
Declared RDF type
skos:Concept
Domain / range
Not applicable to this concept. Domain and range describe predicates; the types, relationships and constraints below describe this resource.

No direct named family-shape reference found. This is not a claim that a profile cannot add constraints or reference this term indirectly.
Cardinality
No universal cardinality declared for this term.
Additional source notes
numcodecs' Delta filter: a running sum over elements of the numpy dtype dtype (i1, u1, [<>|]i2/i4/i8, u2/u4/u8, f4, f8), integers wrapping and floats in their own precision; astype, when given, must equal dtype.

IRI: https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding#XZ

Label
XZ
Definition
The .xz container of LZMA2-filtered blocks with a per-stream integrity check, as the xz tool writes it.
Declared RDF type
skos:Concept
Domain / range
Not applicable to this concept. Domain and range describe predicates; the types, relationships and constraints below describe this resource.
Concept scheme
menc:CompressionScheme
Defined by
<https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding>
Shape usage / validation context
No direct named family-shape reference found. This is not a claim that a profile cannot add constraints or reference this term indirectly.
Cardinality
No universal cardinality declared for this term.
Additional source notes
The .xz container: one or more streams of LZMA2-filtered blocks with an integrity check (CRC32, CRC64 or SHA-256). menc:LZMA names the algorithm family, not this framing.
Notes / examples
"TIFF compression 34925 and numcodecs' LZMA compressor store this container. Parameters: concatenated (1, the default, decodes every stream with stream padding as the xz tool does; 0 decodes the first stream and ignores what follows, as libtiff and GDAL's Zarr reader do), level (0-9, used when encoding) and memoryLimitKiB (the decoder memory a stream may need, default 73728)."@en
Canonical source

menc:YCbCrToRGB — YCbCr to RGB

IRI: https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding#YCbCrToRGB

Label
YCbCr to RGB
Definition
A sample transform converting 8-bit YCbCr samples to RGB with stated luma coefficients, reference black and white levels and chroma subsampling.
Declared RDF type
skos:Concept
Domain / range
Not applicable to this concept. Domain and range describe predicates; the types, relationships and constraints below describe this resource.
Concept scheme
menc:TransformScheme
Defined by
<https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding>
Shape usage / validation context
No direct named family-shape reference found. This is not a claim that a profile cannot add constraints or reference this term indirectly.
Cardinality
No universal cardinality declared for this term.
Additional source notes
Converts 8-bit YCbCr samples to RGB with libtiff's integer tables (tif_color.c) for the given coefficients and reference black/white. layout pixels takes three interleaved samples per pixel; units takes TIFF YCbCr sampling units (subsampling luma samples then Cb and Cr); planes takes the three component planes of a raw JPEG decode.
Canonical source
media-encoding.ttl

  • hexplain:status: hexplain:statusValid

zlib

RFC 1950 zlib framing around DEFLATE, including its header and Adler-32 checksum. Distinct from raw DEFLATE and gzip framing.

Canonical vocabulary and shapes

# Hexplain Register — Media Encoding 1.2
# Controlled values for the hx-encoding aspect: codec and compression identifiers as
# published by the relevant standards bodies (ISO/IEC MPEG, ITU-T, IETF). Split out of the
# aspect so another jurisdiction or profile can publish a sibling register without forking
# the aspect or minting into a namespace it does not own.
# Codes are NOT here: a raw wire code (e.g. a fourCC) belongs to the format that encodes it,
# and is carried by that profile's HDL enum.
@prefix menc: <https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding#> .
@prefix owl:    <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix rdfs:   <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .
@prefix xsd:    <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .
@prefix skos:   <http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#> .
@prefix dcterms:<http://purl.org/dc/terms/> .
@prefix vann:   <http://purl.org/vocab/vann/> .
@prefix hexplain: <https://hexplain.io/ns/core#> .

<https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding> a owl:Ontology ;
    rdfs:seeAlso <https://hexplain.io/specification/register/media-encoding/media-encoding-doc.ttl> ;
    owl:versionIRI <https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding/1.2> ; owl:versionInfo "1.2" ;
    owl:priorVersion <https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding/1.1> ;
    dcterms:modified "2026-10-13"^^xsd:date ;
    # core supplies the codec contract vocabulary the minimum-set concepts carry below.
    owl:imports <https://hexplain.io/ns/core> ;
    rdfs:label "Hexplain Register — Media Encoding"@en ;
    dcterms:created "2026-08-08"^^xsd:date ;
    dcterms:creator <https://geoknoesis.com> ;
    dcterms:license <https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/> ;
    vann:preferredNamespacePrefix "menc" ;
    vann:preferredNamespaceUri "https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding#" .

# ---------- Codec Register (SKOS) ----------
# Open, extensible. Grouped for convenience into video and audio collections.
menc:CodecScheme a skos:ConceptScheme ; rdfs:isDefinedBy <https://hexplain.io/ns/register/media-encoding> ;
    skos:prefLabel "Codec Register"@en .

menc:VideoCodecs a skos:Collection ; skos:prefLabel "Video codecs"@en ;
    skos:member menc:H264 , menc:HEVC , menc:ProRes , menc:VP9 , menc:AV1 .
menc:AudioCodecs a skos:Collection ; skos:prefLabel "Audio codecs"@en ;
    skos:member menc:AAC , menc:MP3 , menc:FLAC , menc:Opus , menc:PCM .
menc:ImageCodecs a skos:Collection ; skos:prefLabel "Image codecs"@en ;
    skos:member menc:JPEG, menc:WebP, menc:JPEGXL, menc:LERC .

menc:H264 a skos:Concept ; skos:definition "Identifier for the H.264/AVC video coding family. Profiles must identify applicable bitstream framing and configuration."@en ; skos:inScheme menc:CodecScheme ; skos:topConceptOf menc:CodecScheme ;
    skos:prefLabel "H.264"@en ; skos:altLabel "AVC"@en , "MPEG-4 Part 10"@en ; rdfs:seeAlso <https://www.iso.org/standard/75400.html> ;
    hexplain:status hexplain:statusValid .
menc:HEVC a skos:Concept ; skos:definition "Identifier for the H.265/HEVC video coding family. Container framing is specified separately."@en ; skos:inScheme menc:CodecScheme ; skos:topConceptOf menc:CodecScheme ;
    skos:prefLabel "H.265"@en ; skos:altLabel "HEVC"@en , "MPEG-H Part 2"@en ;
    hexplain:status hexplain:statusValid .
menc:ProRes a skos:Concept ; skos:definition "Identifier for the ProRes video coding family; the concrete variant belongs to the format/profile metadata."@en ; skos:inScheme menc:CodecScheme ; skos:topConceptOf menc:CodecScheme ;
    skos:prefLabel "Apple ProRes"@en ; skos:altLabel "ProRes"@en ;
    hexplain:status hexplain:statusValid .

    skos:prefLabel "VP9"@en ;
    hexplain:status hexplain:statusValid .
menc:AV1 a skos:Concept ; skos:definition "Identifier for the AV1 video coding format; container and configuration details remain separate."@en ; skos:inScheme menc:CodecScheme ; skos:topConceptOf menc:CodecScheme ;
    skos:prefLabel "AV1"@en ;
    hexplain:status hexplain:statusValid .
menc:AAC a skos:Concept ; skos:definition "Identifier for the Advanced Audio Coding family; profiles specify the object type and framing when required."@en ; skos:inScheme menc:CodecScheme ; skos:topConceptOf menc:CodecScheme ;
    skos:prefLabel "AAC"@en ; skos:altLabel "Advanced Audio Coding"@en ;
    hexplain:status hexplain:statusValid .
menc:MP3 a skos:Concept ; skos:definition "Identifier for MPEG audio Layer III coded audio, distinct from a file's metadata-tag container."@en ; skos:inScheme menc:CodecScheme ; skos:topConceptOf menc:CodecScheme ;
    skos:prefLabel "MP3"@en ; skos:altLabel "MPEG-1 Audio Layer III"@en ;
    hexplain:status hexplain:statusValid .

    skos:prefLabel "FLAC"@en ; skos:altLabel "Free Lossless Audio Codec"@en ;
    hexplain:status hexplain:statusValid .

# The raw block format carries no length and no magic, so it cannot share the frame's concept:
# a decoder has to be told both that it is looking at a bare block and how long its output is.

    skos:note "One LZ4 compressed block, without the frame format's header. Its decoded length is not recorded in the data, so a pipeline step names it with the parameter decodedSize (Processing Model, Codecs beyond the minimum set)."@en ;
    hexplain:status hexplain:statusValid .

    hexplain:status hexplain:statusValid .

    skos:note "A meta-compressor: it wraps an inner codec and its own shuffle and level settings, which travel as hexplain:codecParameter on the step."@en ;

# Reversible TRANSFORMS rather than compressors. They shrink nothing on their own; they
# rearrange bytes so that the compressor after them does better, and they only ever appear
# as a step of a pipeline. They live in this register because that is what a pipeline step
# draws from -- the alternative, a parallel filter register, would split one list in two.
menc:Delta a skos:Concept ; skos:definition "A predictive transform that represents values by differences from preceding or predicted values; the variant and arithmetic are profile parameters."@en ; skos:inScheme menc:CompressionScheme ; skos:topConceptOf menc:CompressionScheme ; skos:prefLabel "Delta / predictor"@en ;
    skos:note "Stores each value as its difference from the previous one. TIFF calls this a horizontal predictor and numbers its variants; the variant travels as a parameter. Its parameters are named by the Processing Model (Codecs, Delta parameters): elementSize (required: 1, 2, 4 or 8), littleEndian (0 or 1), samplesPerPixel (default 1), rowBytes (0 or a multiple of elementSize x samplesPerPixel) and predictor (2, horizontal, by default; 1, none)."@en ;
    hexplain:status hexplain:statusValid .
menc:Shuffle a skos:Concept ; skos:definition "A reversible transform grouping corresponding bytes of successive fixed-width values to prepare them for compression."@en ; skos:inScheme menc:CompressionScheme ; skos:topConceptOf menc:CompressionScheme ; skos:prefLabel "Byte shuffle"@en ;
    skos:note "Groups the Nth byte of every value together, so the high bytes of similar numbers become runs. HDF5 and Blosc both use it."@en ;
    hexplain:status hexplain:statusValid .
menc:BitShuffle a skos:Concept ; skos:definition "A reversible transform grouping corresponding bits of successive values to prepare them for compression; element/block parameters belong to the profile."@en ; skos:inScheme menc:CompressionScheme ; skos:topConceptOf menc:CompressionScheme ; skos:prefLabel "Bit shuffle"@en ;
    hexplain:status hexplain:statusValid .
menc:BloscLZ a skos:Concept ; skos:inScheme menc:CompressionScheme ; skos:topConceptOf menc:CompressionScheme ; skos:prefLabel "BloscLZ"@en ;
    rdfs:comment "c-blosc's BloscLZ compressor (c-blosc 1.21.6 blosclz_decompress): literal runs of 1-32 bytes and matches at a 13-bit distance or, escaped, beyond 8191. Decodes at most decodedSize bytes; as in C, a match that is the stream's last instruction is not copied."@en ;
    hexplain:status hexplain:statusValid ;
    skos:definition "The LZ77-style block compressor built into the Blosc framework, with 13-bit match distances and escaped longer ones."@en .
menc:TypedDelta a skos:Concept ; skos:inScheme menc:TransformScheme ; skos:topConceptOf menc:TransformScheme ; skos:prefLabel "Typed delta"@en ;
    rdfs:comment "numcodecs' Delta filter: a running sum over elements of the numpy dtype dtype (i1, u1, [<>|]i2/i4/i8, u2/u4/u8, f4, f8), integers wrapping and floats in their own precision; astype, when given, must equal dtype."@en ;
    hexplain:status hexplain:statusValid ;
    skos:definition "A reversible transform storing each element of a typed numeric array as its difference from the previous one, undone by a running sum in that type."@en .
menc:FixedScaleOffset a skos:Concept ; skos:inScheme menc:TransformScheme ; skos:topConceptOf menc:TransformScheme ; skos:prefLabel "Fixed scale and offset"@en ;
    rdfs:comment "numcodecs' FixedScaleOffset filter decoded as GDAL does: astype values widened to dtype, then value / scale + offset in double precision (rounded to binary32 for <f4)."@en ;
    hexplain:status hexplain:statusValid ;
    skos:definition "A sample transform that restores floating-point values from samples stored as a narrower integer type by dividing by a fixed scale and adding an offset."@en .
menc:Quantize a skos:Concept ; skos:inScheme menc:TransformScheme ; skos:topConceptOf menc:TransformScheme ; skos:prefLabel "Quantize"@en ;
    rdfs:comment "numcodecs' Quantize filter: decoding converts astype floats to dtype floats; digits is the precision the encoder kept."@en ;
    hexplain:status hexplain:statusValid ;
    skos:definition "A lossy floating-point filter that rounds samples to a fixed number of significant decimal digits when encoding; decoding converts the stored type back."@en .

    rdfs:comment "numcodecs' BitRound filter, which rounds float mantissas to keepbits bits when encoding; decoding is the identity."@en ;
    hexplain:status hexplain:statusValid ;

    rdfs:comment "SGI's LogL run-length coding (TIFF compression 34676): each row stored as its high then low byte plane, each run-length coded; decodes to the 16-bit log-luminance codes, little-endian, as libtiff's SGILOGDATAFMT_16BIT returns them."@en ;

# ---------- Codec contracts: the Processing Model's minimum set ----------
# Every contract and parameter spec is a named resource: <Concept>.contract and
# <Concept>.contract.<parameterKey>. As blank nodes they were minted afresh by every parse, so a
# graph holding the register twice (a processor's bundled copy beside the published one) stated
# two contracts per concept and broke hexplain:CodecContractBindingShape. Named, a repeated load
# adds nothing.
menc:Store hexplain:codecContract menc:Store.contract .
menc:Store.contract a owl:NamedIndividual, hexplain:CodecContract ;
    hexplain:contractVersion 1 ; hexplain:vectorSuiteVersion 1 ;
    hexplain:codecKind hexplain:BytesToBytes ; hexplain:keepsSession false ; hexplain:encodes true ;
    hexplain:raisesCategory "ResourceLimit" .
menc:Deflate hexplain:codecContract menc:Deflate.contract .
menc:Deflate.contract a owl:NamedIndividual, hexplain:CodecContract ;
    hexplain:contractVersion 1 ; hexplain:vectorSuiteVersion 1 ;
    hexplain:codecKind hexplain:BytesToBytes ; hexplain:keepsSession false ; hexplain:encodes true ;
    hexplain:raisesCategory "Validation", "ResourceLimit" ;
    hexplain:declaresParameter menc:Deflate.contract.level .
menc:Deflate.contract.level a owl:NamedIndividual, hexplain:CodecParameterSpec ; hexplain:parameterKey "level" ;
    hexplain:parameterType hexplain:IntegerParameterType ; hexplain:defaultValue -1 ;
    hexplain:minimumValue -1 ; hexplain:maximumValue 9 .
menc:Zlib hexplain:codecContract menc:Zlib.contract .
menc:Zlib.contract a owl:NamedIndividual, hexplain:CodecContract ;
    hexplain:contractVersion 1 ; hexplain:vectorSuiteVersion 1 ;
    hexplain:codecKind hexplain:BytesToBytes ; hexplain:keepsSession false ; hexplain:encodes true ;
    hexplain:raisesCategory "Validation", "Checksum", "ResourceLimit" ;
    hexplain:declaresParameter menc:Zlib.contract.level ,
        menc:Zlib.contract.trailing .
menc:Zlib.contract.level a owl:NamedIndividual, hexplain:CodecParameterSpec ; hexplain:parameterKey "level" ;
    hexplain:parameterType hexplain:IntegerParameterType ; hexplain:defaultValue -1 ;
    hexplain:minimumValue -1 ; hexplain:maximumValue 9 .
menc:Zlib.contract.trailing a owl:NamedIndividual, hexplain:CodecParameterSpec ; hexplain:parameterKey "trailing" ;
    hexplain:parameterType hexplain:StringParameterType ; hexplain:defaultValue "refuse" ;
    hexplain:allowedValue "refuse", "ignore" .
menc:Gzip hexplain:codecContract menc:Gzip.contract .
menc:Gzip.contract a owl:NamedIndividual, hexplain:CodecContract ;
    hexplain:contractVersion 1 ; hexplain:vectorSuiteVersion 1 ;
    hexplain:codecKind hexplain:BytesToBytes ; hexplain:keepsSession false ; hexplain:encodes true ;
    hexplain:raisesCategory "Validation", "Checksum", "ResourceLimit" ;
    hexplain:declaresParameter menc:Gzip.contract.trailing .
menc:Gzip.contract.trailing a owl:NamedIndividual, hexplain:CodecParameterSpec ; hexplain:parameterKey "trailing" ;
    hexplain:parameterType hexplain:StringParameterType ; hexplain:defaultValue "refuse" ;
    hexplain:allowedValue "refuse", "ignore" .
menc:Delta hexplain:codecContract menc:Delta.contract .
menc:Delta.contract a owl:NamedIndividual, hexplain:CodecContract ;
    hexplain:contractVersion 1 ; hexplain:vectorSuiteVersion 1 ;
    hexplain:codecKind hexplain:BytesToBytes ; hexplain:keepsSession false ; hexplain:encodes true ;
    hexplain:raisesCategory "Validation", "Unsupported", "ResourceLimit", "Description" ;
    hexplain:declaresParameter menc:Delta.contract.elementSize ,
        menc:Delta.contract.predictor ,
        menc:Delta.contract.littleEndian ,
        menc:Delta.contract.samplesPerPixel ,
        menc:Delta.contract.rowBytes .
menc:Delta.contract.elementSize a owl:NamedIndividual, hexplain:CodecParameterSpec ; hexplain:parameterKey "elementSize" ;
    hexplain:parameterType hexplain:IntegerParameterType ; hexplain:requiredParameter true ;
    hexplain:allowedValue 1, 2, 4, 8 .
menc:Delta.contract.predictor a owl:NamedIndividual, hexplain:CodecParameterSpec ; hexplain:parameterKey "predictor" ;
    hexplain:parameterType hexplain:IntegerParameterType ; hexplain:defaultValue 2 ;
    hexplain:minimumValue 1 .
menc:Delta.contract.littleEndian a owl:NamedIndividual, hexplain:CodecParameterSpec ; hexplain:parameterKey "littleEndian" ;
    hexplain:parameterType hexplain:IntegerParameterType ; hexplain:defaultValue 0 ;
    hexplain:allowedValue 0, 1 .
menc:Delta.contract.samplesPerPixel a owl:NamedIndividual, hexplain:CodecParameterSpec ; hexplain:parameterKey "samplesPerPixel" ;
    hexplain:parameterType hexplain:IntegerParameterType ; hexplain:defaultValue 1 ;
    hexplain:minimumValue 1 .
menc:Delta.contract.rowBytes a owl:NamedIndividual, hexplain:CodecParameterSpec ; hexplain:parameterKey "rowBytes" ;
